# Controller, service and repository flow

**CURRENT_VERIFIED — source baseline `cdeb1ca1037744d06995058af13d0582f6273c0a`, `huy-review`, 2026-10-13.** Source observation is not all-path runtime execution. Expected business behavior uses owner-confirmed decisions and assigned specs; implementation/test evidence has its own limits.

Controllers bind HTTP/auth actor/version/idempotency and map results; services validate/orchestrate; repositories apply scope/state/evidence and transactional persistence. Some current operations use direct service concrete types or repository orchestration. This is observed architecture, not a claim every route has the same depth. Complete operation closures are in API-to-source matrix and machine map.
